### [non-spec.8.review-mechanism.1]

Lens: end-to-end mechanism. Result: no finding met the bar. I read the round-7-to-8 diff ("stamps no drain request", the S22 dependency on S13, and the new summary defect entries) and traced the D16, CODE-8, CODE-10, CODE-11, and decision-12 flows against the tree.

FACT: `Binder.Resume` runs `connect`, which is `Claimer.Claim`, on every pool, concurrent pools included. Only after that does `reserveResumeSlot` reserve a slot when `MaxConcurrentSessions > 1`. A concurrent resume therefore reaches `Claimer.Claim` with `ResumeRequest.KeepsRuntime` true from `match`. That is consistent with D16, because `KeepsRuntimeAcrossSessions` is true for `maxConcurrentSessions > 1`. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/resume.go:121, :203-206.
FACT: In TEST-21's first tier-9 subtest, no acquisition reads A's pod `idle`, because B's claim runs `endReservedHolds` while A's Sandbox still projects `reserved`. A's pod reaches `draining` through CODE-10's `DrainPinned` with a zero bound (admin-API pool, minWarm = maxWarm = warmCount), not through the gateway stamp. The subtest asserts only `draining`, so it holds. Do not add a gateway-record assertion there. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podclaim/claimer.go:118-126; pkg/controller/poolscaling/poolstoresource.go:107-108.

TEST-21 subtest 1's "sets gateway.claimHoldTTLSeconds long enough" therefore means a gateway env patch and a rollout on the shared Kind cluster. TEST-11 works inside the 10 s default. I did not file this; the implementor should prefer the default. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/flags.go:542-544; tests/tier9_security/tenant_isolation_test.go:51.
FACT: CODE-3 removes the generation reset for every transport, including `SubprocessExecutor`, which spawns a child for each session. On `lenny-adapter --runtime-bin`, a later session's intra-pod MCP therefore fails closed even though its process is fresh. That mode is the single-host developer loop with no pod, so I did not file it. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-adapter/main.go:21-23, :360-366.
FACT: The TEST-16 `TestPerSessionProcessStatementsRemovedFromStagedSites` phrase list was run against the current spec/, docs/ (.md, .svg), and schemas/ (.json) with whitespace collapsed. Every hit is a site that SPEC-3, SPEC-5, SPEC-11 to SPEC-14, CODE-9, DOC-5, or DOC-6 stages, so the gate has no unstaged trip site today.
FACT: The mirror's `state` column is the Sandbox phase (`observedPhase`), so a `reserved` pod never shows as an idle mirror row, and the fallback's `ClaimIdle` cannot select it. EVIDENCE: pkg/controller/warmpool/controller.go:446-470.

- FACT: `DeleteOnHoldExpiry` treats a NotFound DELETE as success and a Conflict as `aborted`, so the acquisition-path hold end and the holder's TTL timer can race on any replica without an error or a double effect. - FACT: the gateway pool store is `pgstore` directly with no cache (`cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go:480-483`), and `poolPolicyMirror.PoolPolicy` returns every non-NotFound error, so `KeepsRuntime` cannot fall back to false on a Postgres outage. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/pool_policy_mirror.go:55-62; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/resolve.go:362-365

### [non-spec.8.review-security.1]

DECISION: no security finding in non-spec round 8 — BECAUSE the delta since spec-r21-prefix is wording ("stamps no drain request" in D16 and SPEC-8(d)), the S22 dependency on S13, summary file lists, and summary defect entries already filed under spec-changes §9.1; none moves a tenant pin, an acknowledgment gate, a credential boundary, or the source of a reuse bound. A full re-walk found every bound on a trusted, durable source — ALTERNATIVES: (1) the swallowed Conflict in `stampPodTenant` (slotclaimer.go:72-74), which CODE-8's fallback inherits, as a path that leaves a served pod unpinned and then admissible to another tenant's kept runtime; rejected, because the patch is a JSON merge patch with no resourceVersion, which the apiserver retries internally instead of returning 409, so the branch is not reachable in practice and it predates this proposal. (2) a CRD-only pool (no pool-store row) routed to `SlotClaimer` with the hard-coded `KeepsRuntime: true`; rejected, because `MaxConcurrentSessions` comes only from the pool-store mirror (resolve.go:369; no CRD field), so a missing row resolves to the `Claimer` path with `KeepsRuntime` false, which fails closed.
FACT: the tenant-label webhook is `failurePolicy: Fail` on Pod CREATE and UPDATE, and `DecideTenantTransition` refuses `{tenant} → unset` (removal), `{A} → {B}`, and `unassigned → {tenant}` for every SA. Only the gateway SA may set the label from unset, and only the WarmPoolController SA may reset it to `unassigned`, which `AdmitTenantPin` refuses for every tenant. So no path makes a served pod read as unpinned. — EVIDENCE: pkg/admission/label_immutability/label_immutability.go:155-209; charts/lenny/templates/admission-policies/label-immutability-webhook.yaml:65-77

A wrapper added later would silently skip the hold-timeout teardown. — EVIDENCE: grep of `.Runtime = ` over pkg/adapter and cmd
FACT: every idle-acquisition call site is one that CODE-8 covers: `CreateClaim` is called only from claimer.go:136 (idle scan), slotclaimer.go:696 (`reserveSlot`, reached from the `ClaimSlot` idle pass), fallbackclaim.go:104, and the unwired podregistry/crd.go:235. `ReserveSlotOnPod` (resume.go:213) requires the claim that `Binder.connect` created through `Claimer.Claim`. — EVIDENCE: grep of `CreateClaim(` and `ReserveSlotOnPod(` over pkg and cmd
WATCHOUT: the fallback re-reads the live Sandbox phase after `CreateClaim` and deletes the claim when the phase is not `idle` (fallbackclaim.go:108-119). D16's argument that the fallback reads phase "from the mirror" holds for the stamp decision only. Do not file it as a false citation: the fallback's claim admission and its stamp decision are separate. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/fallbackclaim.go:91-128

### [non-spec.10.review-security.1]

CORRECTS [UNVERIFIED `sessions_served` source]: settled. The gateway increments `sessions_served` only on receipt of the adapter's `ReportSessionScrub` (`pkg/gateway/mcpfabric/delegationtree/leasecontrol/scrubreport_server.go:461`). The adapter's `reportSessionScrub` is the sole production writer, and it is best-effort: a transport failure is logged and the release goes on (`pkg/adapter/sessionscrubreporter.go:48`, `:52`). The count is therefore an in-pod adapter report, and a lost report under-counts toward `maxSessionsPerPod`. Not filed: the source predates this proposal and the proposal does not change it, the adapter is platform code that the runtime cannot rewrite, and the overrun stays inside one tenant's pinned pod on an acknowledged pool.
FACT: the pool policy mirror reads the pool store directly on every resolution, with no cache (`pkg/gateway/sessionserver/pool_policy_mirror.go:55-60`), and `MaxConcurrentSessions` is taken only from that mirror (`pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/resolve.go:369`). A pool with no row therefore resolves both `MaxConcurrentSessions` 0 and `KeepsRuntime` false, so the slot-path `KeepsRuntime: true` constant never pairs with a row-less pool. EVIDENCE: resolve.go:358-369.
FACT: Decide's `VMRestart`, CODE-11's admission predicate, and `KeepsRuntimeAcrossSessions` all read `scrubProfile` from the same pool-store row (`pkg/gateway/session/recycle/scrubreporter_seams.go:607`, `:639`), so the predicates do not drift between CRD and store. `foldPoolPolicy` ORs in a CRD-derived `Recycle`, but that only sends a pod down the recycle path. `InspectForRecycle` then finds no policy and the missing-report timer retires the pod, so the result fails closed. EVIDENCE: resolve.go:376.
FACT: the tenant-label webhook allows an unchanged label (`label_immutability.go` `DecideTenantTransition`, `oldTenant == newTenant`), so a same-tenant re-acquisition's `stampPodTenant` patch is admitted on Kind. EVIDENCE: pkg/admission/label_immutability/label_immutability.go:165-167.
DECISION: no security finding this round. BECAUSE the only production `ClaimRequest` path is `Binder.connect` (claimconnect.go:57), `SlotClaimer` pass 1 checks `claim.Spec.TenantID` (slotclaimer.go:425), every pool-store SQL write goes through `validatePool` except the resume-epoch bump, and the gateway RBAC already grants every verb CODE-8 uses (gateway-deployment.yaml:161-162). ALTERNATIVES: filing the `sessions_served` trust boundary was rejected for the reasons above.

### [non-spec.10.review-test-coverage.1]
- DECISION: filed one finding. No staged test discriminates the `KeepsRuntime: true` constant on `SlotClaimer.ClaimSlot`'s new `AdmitTenantPin` call. TEST-12's only ClaimSlot case (non-spec-changes.md:205) puts an other-tenant pinned pod ahead of an unpinned one, and that pod is refused under either value. ClaimSlot never stamps a drain, so "writes no annotation" does not discriminate either. The remedy is to extend that case with an idle Pod pinned to the request's own tenant, sorted first, and assert that ClaimSlot binds it — BECAUSE a zero value refuses every same-tenant pinned idle pod on a concurrent pool after its reserved hold ends, against SPEC-8(c) and edge row 77 — ALTERNATIVES: (1) a ClaimSlot Pod-`Get`-error case: rejected, because the tenant-label webhook plus reserveSlot's claim delete on a stamp failure is a backstop. (2) A test of `endReservedHolds` on another tenant's `reserved` hold: rejected, because that tenant's own next acquisition reads `KeepsRuntime` false too, so nothing is dispatched into the kept runtime. (3) Re-filing the sibling-`Start`-after-last-slot-`Interrupt` case from the caller directive: rejected, because history/non-spec.r7 and non-spec-recheck.r1 refuted it on materiality and this lens has no new evidence.
- FACT: no existing ClaimSlot or slot-binder test creates a Pod object for an idle Sandbox. `slotclaimer_test.go` seeds only Sandboxes. The only Pod helper in `slotbinder_test.go` is `drainAgentPod`, which carries no tenant label. Under CODE-8 every existing concurrent-path test therefore takes the absent-Pod admit branch and is blind to the `KeepsRuntime` value — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podclaim/slotclaimer_test.go:95-109; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotbinder_test.go:1028-1039
- FACT: concurrent pods hold `reserved` claims, through `rebindReservedSlot` in ClaimSlot pass 1, so after the hold ends a concurrent pod returns to `idle` pinned and becomes a pass-2 `AdmitTenantPin` candidate — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podclaim/slotclaimer.go:443-453, :476-506
- WATCHOUT: 0078's TEST-5 (tier 4, `TestAdapterServesTwoSequentialSessionsOverOneRuntimeSocket_spec_15_4_3`) still passes under CODE-1. The second `Start` returns nil on the kept connection, and echocore answers any number of sessions until EOF or `shutdown`. Only 0078's doc sentence "the second session's runtime comes from SpawnPath" becomes false, and 0078 owns that text — EVIDENCE: pkg/runtimekit/echocore/echocore.go:48-86; proposals/0078_*/0078_*.non-spec-changes.md:432-439
- USEFUL [non-spec.8.review-test-coverage.1]: its Resume-hop finding gave the template for this one. A `KeepsRuntime` value that one call site sets as a literal or copies is untested unless a case discriminates both values at that site.

### [non-spec.12.fix-G1.1]
DECISION: bound the Postgres fallback's lock window by wrapping only `b.Fallback.ClaimIdle` in `fallbackClaim` with `context.WithTimeout(Binder.FallbackClaimTimeout)` (zero means `DefaultFallbackClaimTimeout`, 5 s; flag `--pod-claim-fallback-timeout-seconds`, env `LENNY_POD_CLAIM_FALLBACK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`), with the admit callback using the ctx `ClaimIdle` hands it — BECAUSE the request ctx carries no deadline, the cluster client sets only QPS/Burst (cmd/lenny-gateway/pod_lifecycle.go:164-165), and `pgstore.Sync` waits on the FOR UPDATE rows before `plan.Compute`; one wrap covers every Get and every Postgres round trip of the window, changes no interface, and keeps decision 9 — ALTERNATIVES: per-Get timeout inside `AdmitTenantPin` (bounds N×bound and touches the unlocked idle-scan/slot sites), `lock_timeout` on Sync (leaves the gateway connection and locks unbounded and stales the mirror), hard-coded constant (code-best-practices requires an override), a new fallback-skip reason (spec-enumerated labels).
WATCHOUT: a callback that closes over the outer request ctx instead of the `AdmitFunc` ctx parameter defeats the bound; CODE-8 states this trap once and TEST-13 **Pin read bounded** is the case that discriminates it. EVIDENCE: non-spec-changes.md CODE-8 fallback bullet.
FACT: `Binder.FallbackMaxMirrorLagSeconds` (binder.go:65) is never set from cmd/lenny-gateway; only its zero-means-default pattern is reused, and `DefaultFallbackMaxMirrorLagSeconds` lives in fallbackclaim.go:30, not binder.go. The new default constant may sit beside it. EVIDENCE: grep of cmd/ for FallbackMaxMirrorLag returns nothing.
FACT: pgx v5.9.2 `dbTx.Rollback` on an expired ctx fails its Exec and calls `conn.die()`, closing the connection, so the server aborts the transaction and the row locks are released without a change to `ClaimIdle`'s `defer tx.Rollback(ctx)`. EVIDENCE: $GOMODCACHE/github.com/jackc/pgx/v5@v5.9.2/tx.go:209-223; pkg/agentpodstate/pgstore/pgstore.go ClaimIdle defer.
DECISION: the new flag is not wired into charts/lenny (no chart deliverable) — BECAUSE the default applies without chart wiring and the env override is the operator path; the design scoped the cascade to flags.go and pod_lifecycle.go — ALTERNATIVES: a values.yaml key like `slotCounterPostgresFallbackMaxSeconds` (charts/lenny/templates/gateway-deployment.yaml) would add a chart deliverable and helm-unittest case for a tuning knob nobody has asked to expose.
FACT: summary.md's CODE-8 index entry lists the packages CODE-8 touches, so adding cmd/lenny-gateway to CODE-8 required adding it there (the design's siteDispositions did not name that line).

### [non-spec.12.fix-design-G1.1]
DECISION: bound the fallback by wrapping only the `b.Fallback.ClaimIdle` call in `fallbackClaim` with `context.WithTimeout(ctx, b.FallbackClaimTimeout)` (new Binder field, zero means `DefaultFallbackClaimTimeout` = 5s, set from a new gateway flag `--pod-claim-fallback-timeout-seconds` / `LENNY_POD_CLAIM_FALLBACK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`); the admit callback passes the ctx `ClaimIdle` gives it to `AdmitTenantPin`; an expired bound returns through the unchanged admit-error path (rollback, wrapped error) — BECAUSE it bounds the whole lock-hold window (every Get plus the Postgres round trips) at one site, changes no interface, and does not touch decision 9 (the read stays inside the transaction) — ALTERNATIVES: (1) per-Get timeout inside `AdmitTenantPin`: bounds N rows x t rather than the loop, and also changes the primary idle-scan and slot paths; (2) `SET LOCAL lock_timeout` on the WPC `Sync`: leaves the gateway pgx connection and FOR UPDATE locks held unbounded, and a failed Sync stales the mirror into the mirror-lag skip; (3) hard-coded constant: violates code-best-practices "a default not fixed by the spec must be overridable"; (4) map the expiry to `ErrNoIdlePod` or a new `lenny_pod_claim_fallback_skipped_total` reason: the reason labels are spec-enumerated (spec/04 §4.6.1 fallback preconditions), so that is spec surface; (5) moving the read out of the transaction: re-opens human decision 9.
WATCHOUT: the callback must call `AdmitTenantPin` with the ctx `ClaimIdle` passes to `AdmitFunc`, never the outer request ctx captured by the closure; a captured outer ctx leaves the Get unbounded and is exactly what the new TEST-13 case catches (the outer test ctx must outlive the bound). EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/fallbackclaim.go:91
FACT: pgx v5.9.2 `dbTx.Rollback` on an expired ctx fails the "rollback" Exec and calls `conn.die()`, so a deadline-expired `ClaimIdle` releases its row locks by closing the connection rather than by a sent ROLLBACK; no `ClaimIdle` change is needed, and the connection is not returned to the pool. EVIDENCE: $GOMODCACHE/github.com/jackc/pgx/v5@v5.9.2/tx.go:209-220; pkg/agentpodstate/pgstore/pgstore.go:254
FACT: the fallback ctx carries no deadline on either pool policy: `waitInQueue` checks its wait bound between attempts and never puts it on the attempt ctx. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/queue.go:178-205
FACT: the spec names `podClaimFallbackMaxMirrorLagSeconds` "configurable via gateway flag" (spec/04_system-components.md:443), but `Binder.FallbackMaxMirrorLagSeconds` has no flag wiring in cmd/lenny-gateway; a pre-existing gap, out of this proposal's scope. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:58-65; cmd/lenny-gateway/pod_lifecycle.go:315-325
WATCHOUT: no docs page or chart value lists gateway flags (`claim-hold-ttl-seconds` appears in neither docs/ nor charts/), so the new flag owes no DOC deliverable; its help string is the operator-tunable documentation. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/flags.go:542-547

### [non-spec.12.review-kubernetes.1]

- FACT: The pool reconciler (`warmpool`) watches SandboxWarmPool, Sandbox (Owns), and PDB only; Pod and SandboxClaim informers exist in the same manager through the `warmpool-pod` reconciler (`For(&corev1.Pod{})`) and the occupancy reconciler (`Watches(&lennyv1.SandboxClaim{})`), so CODE-10's cached Pod List and SandboxClaim Get hit a populated cache. A pinned-idle change that arrives with no Sandbox event (claim deleted before the first bound patch) is caught by the 60 s `IdleMeterInterval` requeue, so the CODE-10 accounting stays level-triggered. — EVIDENCE: pkg/controller/warmpool/controller.go:998-1004, pkg/controller/warmpool/pod_reconciler.go:758, pkg/controller/warmpool/occupancy.go:308, controller.go:331-336
- FACT: SandboxClaims carry no finalizer (the only WPC finalizer is `FinalizerSessionCleanup` on Sandboxes), so the D16 guard "stamp only after a direct `Get` of `claim-<name>` returns NotFound" cannot be held open by a terminating claim. — EVIDENCE: pkg/controller/warmpool/controller.go:520
- FACT: The pool-config webhook cannot evaluate the CODE-11 rule because the SandboxTemplate CRD carries only `recycle.scrubProfile` and `acknowledgeMicrovmResidualState` (no `enabled`, `maxSessionsPerPod`, or `acknowledgeProcessLevelIsolation`); gateway poolstore validation is the gate, matching the existing concurrent-session acknowledgment. — EVIDENCE: pkg/apis/lenny/v1alpha1/sandboxtemplate_types.go:33-66, pkg/admission/pool_config_validator/validator.go:383-392
- DECISION: Kubernetes-idiom lens returns no finding in round 12 — BECAUSE every new write has its owner (gateway: Pod tenant label and drain-request annotation, SandboxClaim DELETE with UID/resourceVersion preconditions, both granted at controller-rbac.yaml and gateway-deployment.yaml:161-162; WPC: Sandbox status via drainSandbox), no status field is used as a gateway-to-controller inbox, no finalizer is added, and the acquisition path never waits on a reconcile — ALTERNATIVES: filing the gateway-stamped drain-request annotation as a command channel was rejected because it is the pre-existing §5.2 mechanism (`StampDrainRequest`) and SPEC-4(d) only widens its stated trigger.

### [non-spec.12.review-reliability.1]

DECISION: filed one finding. The fallback's in-transaction `AdmitTenantPin` Pod Get (non-spec §4.4, CODE-8 fallback bullet) runs with the request ctx and no deadline, while `ClaimIdle` holds a gateway pgx connection and `FOR UPDATE` row locks on every offered idle row. The WarmPoolController's `syncMirror` runs synchronously in `Reconcile` before the plan and upserts every observed row of the pool in one transaction, so it waits on those locks. With the default single reconcile worker, that wait stalls replenishment, drains, and status for every pool. The fallback runs in the apiserver-degraded mode that makes the Gets slow — BECAUSE the gateway rest config sets only QPS and Burst (no Timeout), no gateway HTTP handler deadline exists, and controller-runtime v0.19.4 sets no reconcile timeout — ALTERNATIVES: (1) moving the read out of the transaction (rejected: decision 9); (2) a `lock_timeout` on the mirror Sync (rejected: the fix belongs at the new call that holds the lock, and the Sync is outside this proposal's file list); (3) re-filing the sibling-`Start` TEST-1 case (rejected: refuted on materiality in history/non-spec.r2 to r7).
FACT: `Reconcile` calls `syncMirror` inline at controller.go:247, before `plan.Compute`, and `pgstore.Sync` upserts every observed row with `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` inside one transaction, which waits on a row another transaction holds `FOR UPDATE`. EVIDENCE: pkg/controller/warmpool/controller.go:241-247, :436-444; pkg/agentpodstate/pgstore/pgstore.go:60-105
FACT: `--max-concurrent-reconciles` defaults to 1 for the WarmPoolController. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-controller/flags.go:166, cmd/lenny-controller/controllers.go:45
FACT: the gateway's cluster client has no request timeout (only QPS/Burst), and the gateway's HTTP servers set only `ReadHeaderTimeout`, so a claim-path apiserver call is bounded only by client disconnect or the apiserver's own request timeout. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/pod_lifecycle.go:164-170; cmd/lenny-gateway/httpsurface.go:981
WATCHOUT: the fallback shares the gateway's main `pgPool` (cmd/lenny-gateway/pod_lifecycle.go:315-316), so every concurrent fallback that is waiting on an apiserver Get also holds one gateway Postgres connection. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go:480-483
USEFUL [non-spec.10.review-reliability.1]: its FACTs on the HoldCoordinator timer and the direct pool-store read closed both re-checks without re-tracing them.

### [non-spec.12.review-security.1]

This settles the Open UNVERIFIED on the `sessions_served` source in favour of non-spec.1.review-security.1. It is pre-existing, bounds only same-tenant reuse (the cross-tenant bound is the gateway-read, webhook-guarded `lenny.dev/tenant-id` pin), and is not a 0079 finding; F-5.2.35 owns the counter. — EVIDENCE: pkg/adapter/sessionscrubreporter.go:47-49; pkg/gateway/mcpfabric/delegationtree/leasecontrol/scrubreport_server.go:455-468
FACT: The tenant-label webhook forbids `{tenant} → unset` as well as `{A} → {B}` and `unassigned → {tenant}`, so no writer can return a served pod to "unpinned"; AdmitTenantPin's admit-on-absent-label branch cannot be reached by a label removal. — EVIDENCE: pkg/admission/label_immutability/label_immutability.go:197-208
FACT: The only production `BindRequest`/`ResumeRequest` literals are `exclusiveBindRequest` and `resume_rebind.go:97`, and both have `match` and `row.TenantID` in scope, so CODE-8's threading covers every exclusive acquisition; `pkg/podregistry/crd.go:235` is the dead `ClaimPod` path the summary already files. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/bind_request.go:146; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/resume_rebind.go:75-100
DECISION: no security finding on the round-11 diff (the TEST-12 `ClaimSlot` bullet and the deleted summary watch-out) — BECAUSE ClaimSlot pass 2 (slotclaimer.go:486-510) skips claimed pods, so a pod pinned to the request's tenant with no claim reaches AdmitTenantPin and binds only under `KeepsRuntime: true`, which is what the bullet pins — ALTERNATIVES: filing a missing ClaimSlot read-error test; rejected because TEST-12 pins the helper's error return and a caller that skips on (false, err) binds nothing.
